- **requirements-rewrite**: Writes requirements that restate accepted ADRs and overlapping guides in current words as a snapshot at a recorded revision, or refreshes an existing snapshot after substantial refactoring or new ADRs. ADRs stay the binding, append-only history, and the repository's CONTEXT.md and ADR workflow are updated to route through the snapshot. Use when the source document mixes business requirements with technical/operational/infrastructure perspectives and needs to be cleaned up first.
